      def fused_conv_relu(x, W, b=None, stride=1, pad=0, dilation=1, visualize=False):
          return FusedConvReLU(stride, pad, dilation, visualize)(x, W, b)
      
      class FusedConvBNReLU(Function):
          """
          铻嶅悎 Conv2d + BatchNorm2d + ReLU 鐨勭畻瀛愩€?
          鍓嶅悜锛氬嵎绉?鈫?鎵归噺褰掍竴鍖?鈫?ReLU
          鍙嶅悜锛歊eLU 姊害 鈫?BN 姊害 鈫?鍗风Н姊害
          """
          def __init__(self, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=(1,1),
                        running_mean=None, running_var=None, momentum=0.9, eps=1e-5, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.stride = pair(stride)
              self.pad = pair(pad)
              self.dilation = pair(dilation)   # 鎵╁紶鍗风Н鍙傛暟
              self.running_mean = running_mean
              self.running_var = running_var
              self.momentum = momentum
              self.eps = eps
              self.visualize = visualize
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              """
              铻嶅悎 Conv2d + BatchNorm2d + ReLU 鐨勫墠鍚戜紶鎾€?
      
              Args:
                  x: 杈撳叆鏁版嵁
                  W: 鏉冮噸
                  b: 鍋忕疆
                  gamma: BN 缂╂斁鍙傛暟
                  beta: BN 鍋忕Щ鍙傛暟
      
              GPU閫傞厤璇存槑:
                  - 浼樺厛浠庢潈閲峎鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡绫诲瀷
                  - 褰揥鏄痗upy鏁扮粍鏃讹紝闇€瑕佸厛灏唜杞崲涓篶upy鏁扮粍
              """
              # 杈撳叆锛歺, W, b, gamma, beta
              x, W, b, gamma, beta = xs
              OC, _, KH, KW = W.shape
      
              # 鐢ㄤ簬铻嶅悎绠楀瓙鏃剁殑鍒濆鍖?
              if not hasattr(self,'outsize'):
                  self.outsize = OC
      
              # 鍏抽敭淇锛氫紭鍏堜粠鏉冮噸W鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡锛岃€岄潪杈撳叆x
              W_data = W.data if isinstance(W, Tensor) else W
              xp = get_array_module(W_data)
      
              # 濡傛灉x涓嶆槸xp绫诲瀷鐨勬暟缁勶紝闇€瑕佸厛杞崲
              # 浣跨敤to_xp杈呭姪鍑芥暟姝ｇ‘澶勭悊numpy/cupy涔嬮棿鐨勮浆鎹?
              x_data = to_xp(x, xp)
      
              # ---------- 1. 鍗风Н ----------
              # im2col
              col = im2col_array(x_data, (KH, KW), self.stride, self.pad, to_matrix=False, dilation=self.dilation, xp=xp)
              # 鍗风Н杈撳嚭 (N, OH, OW, OC)
              conv_out = xp.tensordot(col, W_data, ((1, 2, 3), (1, 2, 3)))
              if b is not None:
                  b_data = to_xp(b, xp)
                  conv_out += b_data
              conv_out = xp.rollaxis(conv_out, 3, 1)
      
              # 淇濆瓨鍗风Н杈撳嚭鍜岃緭鍏ワ紝鐢ㄤ簬鍙嶅悜
              self.x = x_data
              self.W = W_data
              self.b = b_data if b is not None else None
              self.conv_out = conv_out
      
              # ---------- 2. 鎵归噺褰掍竴鍖?----------
              # 璁＄畻鍧囧€煎拰鏂瑰樊 (鍦?(N, H, W) 缁村害涓?
              mean = conv_out.mean(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=True)   # (1, OC, 1, 1)
              var = conv_out.var(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=True)     # (1, OC, 1, 1)
      
              # 璁粌鏃舵洿鏂?running 缁熻閲?
              if Config.train:
                  m = mean.reshape(OC)
                  v = var.reshape(OC)
      
                  if self.running_mean is None:
                      self.running_mean = Tensor(xp.zeros(OC, dtype='float32'), requires_grad=False, name='running_mean')
                  if self.running_var is None:
                      self.running_var = Tensor(xp.ones(OC, dtype='float32'), requires_grad=False, name='running_mean')
      
                  self.running_mean.data = self.momentum * self.running_mean.data + (1 - self.momentum) * m
                  self.running_var.data  = self.momentum * self.running_var.data  + (1 - self.momentum) * v
                  self.mean = mean
                  self.var = var
              else:
                  # 娴嬭瘯妯″紡锛氫娇鐢?running 缁熻閲?
                  if self.running_mean is None:
                      self.running_mean = Tensor(xp.zeros(OC, dtype='float32'), requires_grad=False, name='running_mean')
                  if self.running_var is None:
                      self.running_var = Tensor(xp.ones(OC, dtype='float32'), requires_grad=False, name='running_mean')
      
                  mean = self.running_mean.data.reshape(1, OC, 1, 1)
                  var = self.running_var.data.reshape(1, OC, 1, 1)
                  self.mean = mean
                  self.var = var
      
              # 褰掍竴鍖栥€佺缉鏀俱€佸钩绉?
              std_inv = 1.0 / xp.sqrt(var + self.eps)
              x_hat = (conv_out - mean) * std_inv
              gamma_reshaped = gamma.reshape(1, OC, 1, 1)
              beta_reshaped  = beta.reshape(1, OC, 1, 1)
              bn_out = gamma_reshaped * x_hat + beta_reshaped
      
              # 淇濆瓨 BN 涓棿鍙橀噺
              self.gamma = gamma
              self.beta = beta
              self.x_hat = x_hat
              self.std_inv = std_inv
      
              # ---------- 3. ReLU ----------
              self.mask = bn_out > 0
              out = bn_out * self.mask
      
              return out
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              # gys: 杈撳嚭姊害 (N, OC, OH, OW)
              # ---------- 1. ReLU 姊害 ----------
              g_relu = gys.data * self.mask   # 瀵?bn_out 鐨勬搴?
      
              # ---------- 2. BN 姊害 ----------
              x = self.conv_out               # 鍗风Н杈撳嚭锛孊N 鐨勮緭鍏?
              gamma = self.gamma.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
              mean = self.mean
              var = self.var
              eps = self.eps
              N, C, H, W = x.shape
              M = N * H * W   # 姣忎釜閫氶亾鐨勫儚绱犳€绘暟
              OC, _, KH, KW = self.W.shape
      
              xp = get_array_module(x)
      
              # 璁＄畻 gamma 鍜?beta 鐨勬搴?
              gbeta = g_relu.sum(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=False)          # (OC,)
              ggamma = (g_relu * self.x_hat).sum(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=False)  # (OC,)
      
              # 瀵?x_hat 鐨勬搴?
              gx_hat = g_relu * gamma
              # 瀵规柟宸拰鍧囧€肩殑姊害
              gvar = (gx_hat * (x - mean) * (-0.5) * self.std_inv**3).sum(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=True)
              gmean = (gx_hat * (-self.std_inv)).sum(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=True) + \
                      gvar * (-2.0 / M) * (x - mean).sum(axis=(0,2,3), keepdims=True)
              # 瀵?BN 杈撳叆锛堝嵆鍗风Н杈撳嚭锛夌殑姊害
              g_conv_out = gx_hat * self.std_inv + gvar * (2.0 / M) * (x - mean) + gmean / M
      
              # ---------- 3. 鍗风Н姊害 ----------
              # 浣跨敤鍗风Н鐨勫弽鍚戜紶鎾叕寮?
              # gW: (OC, C, KH, KW)
              col_x = im2col_array(self.x, (KH, KW), self.stride, self.pad, to_matrix=False, dilation=self.dilation)
              gW = xp.tensordot(g_conv_out, col_x, ((0,2,3), (0,4,5)))   # (OC, C, KH, KW)
      
              # gb (濡傛灉鏈夊亸缃?
              gb = None
              if self.b is not None:
                  gb = g_conv_out.sum(axis=(0,2,3))
      
              # gx
              gx = conv2d_backward_input_array(
                  g_conv_out, self.W,
                  stride=self.stride,
                  pad=self.pad,
                  dilation=self.dilation,
                  out_h=self.x.shape[2],
                  out_w=self.x.shape[3],
              )
      
              # 杩斿洖姊害锛岄『搴忎笌 forward 杈撳叆涓€鑷?
              # 杩斿洖锛歡x, gW, gb, ggamma, gbeta
              return (as_Tensor(gx), as_Tensor(gW),
                      as_Tensor(gb) if gb is not None else None,
                      as_Tensor(ggamma),
                      as_Tensor(gbeta)
                      )
      
      def fused_conv_bn_relu(x, W, b, gamma, beta, running_mean, running_var, stride=1, pad=0, dilation=1, momentum=0.9, eps=1e-5, visualize=False):
          return FusedConvBNReLU(stride, pad, dilation, running_mean, running_var, momentum, eps, visualize)(x, W, b, gamma, beta)
      
      class CastRegistry:
          '''
          娉ㄥ唽鑷姩绮惧害
          resist_cast: 浠嶤onfig.current_dtype鎻愰珮鍒癴loat32
          cancast: 闄嶄綆绮惧害鑷矯onfig.current_dtype (涓€鑸槸float16)
          '''
          cancast = [
              Tanh,
              MatMul,
              Linear,
              Sigmoid,
              ReLU,
              Conv2d,
              GroupedConv2d,
              Deconv2d,
              Pooling,
              AveragePooling,
              GlobalAveragePooling,
              FusedConvReLU
          ]
      
          resist_cast = [
              Sin,
              Cos,
              Exp,
              Log,
              Mean,
              Softmax,
              BatchNorm2d,
              FusedConvBNReLU
          ]
      
      def type_precedence(dtype):
          order = {'b':0, 'i':1, 'u':2, 'f':3, 'c':4}
          return (order[dtype.kind], dtype.itemsize)
      
      def compare_dtype_greater(dtype1, dtype2):
          if isinstance(dtype1, str):
              dtype1 = np.dtype(dtype1)
          if isinstance(dtype2, str):
              dtype2 = np.dtype(dtype2)
      
          return True if type_precedence(dtype1) > type_precedence(dtype2) else False
      
      def compare_dtype_equal(dtype1, dtype2):
          if isinstance(dtype1, str):
              dtype1 = np.dtype(dtype1)
          if isinstance(dtype2, str):
              dtype2 = np.dtype(dtype2)
      
          return True if type_precedence(dtype1) == type_precedence(dtype2) else False
      
      class Cast(Function):
          def __init__(self, dtype='float16'):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              if len(xs) > 1:
                  raise ValueError("Cast function only supports single input.")
              x = xs[0]
              # 鏀瑰彉绮惧害
              x = x.astype(self.dtype) if not compare_dtype_equal(x.dtype, self.dtype) else x
              return x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return gys
      
      def cast(x):
          return Cast()(x)
      
      
      class DownCast(Function):
          def __init__(self, dtype='float16'):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              if len(xs) > 1:
                  raise ValueError("DownCast function only supports single input.")
              x = xs[0]
              # 闄嶄綆绮惧害
              x = x.astype(self.dtype) if compare_dtype_greater(x.dtype, self.dtype) else x
              return x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return gys
      
      def downcast(x):
          return DownCast()(x)
      
      class UpCast(Function):
          def __init__(self, dtype='float32'):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              if len(xs) > 1:
                  raise ValueError("UpCast function only supports single input.")
              x = xs[0]
              # 鎻愰珮绮惧害
              x = x.astype(self.dtype) if compare_dtype_greater(self.dtype, x.dtype) else x
              return x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return gys
      
      def upcast(x):
          return UpCast()(x)
      
      def get_qmin_qmax(dtype):
          if type(dtype) == str and dtype.startswith('int'):
              num_bits = int(dtype[3:])
              qmin = -(2 ** (num_bits - 1))
              qmax = 2 ** (num_bits - 1) - 1
          elif type(dtype) == str and dtype.startswith('uint'):
              num_bits = int(dtype[4:])
              qmin = 0
              qmax = 2 ** num_bits - 1
          elif isinstance(dtype, np.dtype):
              qmin = np.iinfo(dtype).min
              qmax = np.iinfo(dtype).max
          elif has_cupy and isinstance(dtype, cp.dtype):
              qmin = cp.iinfo(dtype).min
              qmax = cp.iinfo(dtype).max
          else:
              raise ValueError(f"Unsupported dtype for quantization: {dtype}")
          return qmin, qmax
      
      class FakeQuantize(Function):
          def __init__(self, dtype='int8', gamma=0.9):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
              self.qmin, self.qmax = get_qmin_qmax(dtype)
              self.gamma = gamma
      
              self.scale = None
              self.zero_point = None
              self.alpha = None
              self.beta = None
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              xp = get_array_module(x)
      
              # 璁＄畻杈撳叆鐨勬渶灏忓€煎拰鏈€澶у€?
              x_min = x.min()
              x_max = x.max()
              # 鏇存柊alpha鍜宐eta
              if self.alpha is None:
                  self.alpha = x_min
              elif x_min < self.alpha:
                  self.alpha = x_min
              else:
                  self.alpha = self.gamma * self.alpha + (1 - self.gamma) * x_min
      
              if self.beta is None:
                  self.beta = x_max
              elif x_max > self.beta:
                  self.beta = x_max
              else:
                  self.beta = self.gamma * self.beta + (1 - self.gamma) * x_max
      
              # 璁＄畻缂╂斁鍥犲瓙
              self.scale = (self.beta - self.alpha) / (self.qmax - self.qmin) if self.beta > self.alpha else 1.0
              self.zero_point = xp.round(self.qmin - (self.alpha / self.scale)).clip(self.qmin, self.qmax) if self.scale > 0 else 0
      
              # 閲忓寲骞跺弽閲忓寲
              q_x = xp.round(x / self.scale + self.zero_point).clip(self.qmin, self.qmax) if self.scale > 0 else x
              fq_x = (q_x - self.zero_point) * self.scale if self.scale > 0 else x
              #print(f'xmin - alpha = {x_min - self.alpha}')
              #print(f'beta - max = {self.beta - x_max}')
              #print(f'average offset = {xp.average(fq_x - x)}')
              return fq_x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              # Straight-Through Estimator: 姊害鐩存帴浼犻€掞紝涓嶈€冭檻閲忓寲璇樊
              return gys
      
      def fake_quantize(x, dtype='int8', gamma=0.9):
          return FakeQuantize(dtype=dtype, gamma=gamma)(x)
      
      class FakeDequantize(Function):
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              # 鍙嶉噺鍖栵細鐩存帴杩斿洖杈撳叆锛屼繚鎸佷笌 FakeQuantize 鐨勮緭鍑轰竴鑷?
              return x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              # Straight-Through Estimator: 姊害鐩存帴浼犻€掞紝涓嶈€冭檻閲忓寲璇樊
              return gys
      
      def fake_dequantize(x):
          return FakeDequantize()(x)
      
      class QuantizeRegistry:
          can_quantize = [
              Conv2d,
              GroupedConv2d,
              Deconv2d,
              MatMul,
              Linear,
              FusedConvReLU
          ]
      
      class Quantize(Function):
          def __init__(self, scale, zero_point, dtype='int8'):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
              self.qmin, self.qmax = get_qmin_qmax(dtype)
              self.scale = scale
              self.zero_point = zero_point
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              xp = get_array_module(x)
      
              # 閲忓寲
              q_x = xp.round(x / self.scale + self.zero_point).clip(self.qmin, self.qmax)
              return q_x.astype(self.dtype)
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return gys
      
      def quantize(x, scale, zero_point, dtype='int8'):
          return Quantize(scale, zero_point, dtype=dtype)(x)
      
      class Dequantize(Function):
          def __init__(self, scale, zero_point, dtype='int8'):
              super().__init__()
              self.dtype = dtype
              self.qmin, self.qmax = get_qmin_qmax(dtype)
              self.scale = scale
              self.zero_point = zero_point
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              # 鍙嶉噺鍖?
              deq_x = (x.astype('float32') - self.zero_point) * self.scale
              return deq_x
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return gys
      
      def dequantize(x, scale, zero_point, dtype='int8'):
          return Dequantize(scale, zero_point, dtype)(x)

      ## 蹇€熷紑濮?
      
      ### 鍩烘湰浣跨敤绀轰緥
      
      ```python
      import numpy as np
      from eneuro.nn import Module, Linear, Sequential
      from eneuro.nn.optim import SGD
      from eneuro.nn.loss import meanSquaredError
      from eneuro.base import Tensor
      
      # 鍒涘缓涓€涓畝鍗曠殑绾挎€фā鍨?
      model = Sequential(
          Linear(10),
          Linear(1)
      )
      
      # 鍒涘缓浼樺寲鍣?
      optimizer = SGD(model.get_params_list(), lr=0.01)
      
      # 鍑嗗璁粌鏁版嵁
      x = Tensor(np.random.randn(100, 10))
      y = Tensor(np.random.randn(100, 1))
      
      # 璁粌妯″瀷
      for epoch in range(100):
          # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
          y_pred = model(x)
          # 璁＄畻鎹熷け
          loss = meanSquaredError(y_pred, y)
          # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
          loss.backward()
          # 鏇存柊鍙傛暟
          optimizer.step()
          # 娓呯┖姊害
          optimizer.zero_grad()
      
          if epoch % 10 == 0:
              print(f'Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {loss.data}')
      ```
      
      ### 鍗风Н绁炵粡缃戠粶绀轰緥
      
      ```python
      from eneuro.nn import Sequential, Conv2d, Linear
      from eneuro.base import relu, pooling, softmax
      
      # 鍒涘缓涓€涓畝鍗曠殑CNN妯″瀷
      model = Sequential(
          Conv2d(32, 3, pad=1),  # 鍗风Н灞?
          relu,                   # 婵€娲诲嚱鏁?
          pooling(2, 2),          # 姹犲寲灞?
          Conv2d(64, 3, pad=1),
          relu,
          pooling(2, 2),
          Linear(128),
          relu,

      ## 鏍稿績妯″潡
      
      ### nn 妯″潡
      
      - **Layer**锛氭墍鏈夌綉缁滃眰鐨勫熀绫?
      - **Module**锛氬彲搴忓垪鍖栫殑缃戠粶妯″潡
      - **Sequential**锛氶『搴忓鍣紝鎸夐『搴忔墽琛岀綉缁滃眰
      - **Linear**锛氱嚎鎬у叏杩炴帴灞?
      - **Conv2d**锛氫簩缁村嵎绉眰
      - **Dropout**锛?dropout灞傦紝鐢ㄤ簬闃叉杩囨嫙鍚?
      - **MLP**锛氬灞傛劅鐭ユ満
      - **CNNWithPooling**锛氬甫鏈夋睜鍖栧眰鐨勫嵎绉缁忕綉缁?
      
      ### loss 妯″潡
      
      - **MSELoss**锛氬潎鏂硅宸崯澶?

      - **SigmoidWithLoss**锛歴igmoid+浜ゅ弶鐔垫崯澶?

      ### optim 妯″潡
      
      - **Optimizer**锛氫紭鍖栧櫒鍩虹被
      - **SGD**锛氶殢鏈烘搴︿笅闄嶄紭鍖栧櫒
      - **MomentumSGD**锛氬甫鍔ㄩ噺鐨勯殢鏈烘搴︿笅闄嶄紭鍖栧櫒

      ### data 妯″潡
      
      - **Dataset**锛氭暟鎹泦鍩虹被

      ### 璁粌MNIST鍒嗙被妯″瀷
      
      ```python
      from eneuro.data import Dataset, DataLoader
      from eneuro.nn import Sequential, Conv2d, Linear
      from eneuro.nn.optim import Adam
      from eneuro.nn.loss import softmaxCrossEntropy
      from eneuro.base import relu, pooling, softmax
      from eneuro.train import Trainer
      
      # 鍑嗗鏁版嵁
      train_dataset = Dataset(...)  # 鍔犺浇MNIST鏁版嵁闆?
      train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=64)
      
      # 鍒涘缓妯″瀷
      model = Sequential(
          Conv2d(32, 3, pad=1),
          relu,
          pooling(2, 2),
          Conv2d(64, 3, pad=1),
          relu,
          pooling(2, 2),
          Linear(128),
          relu,
          Linear(10),
          softmax
      )
      
      # 鍒涘缓浼樺寲鍣?
      optimizer = Adam(model.get_params_list(), lr=0.001)
      
      # 鍒涘缓璁粌鍣?
      trainer = Trainer(model, optimizer, softmaxCrossEntropy)
      
      # 璁粌妯″瀷
      trainer.train(train_loader, epochs=10, validate=True)
      ```

      # 鍗风Н妯″潡鏍稿績浠ｇ爜浼唬鐮?
      
      ## 1. 鏅€氬嵎绉?(Conv2d)
      
      ```python
      class Conv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0,
                       nobias=False, in_channels=None):
              self.in_channels = in_channels
              self.out_channels = out_channels
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
      
              self.W = Parameter(None, name='W')
              if in_channels is not None:
                  self._init_W()
      
              self.b = None if nobias else Parameter(np.zeros(out_channels), name='b')
      
          def _init_W(self):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
              scale = np.sqrt(1 / (C * KH * KW))
              self.W.data = np.random.randn(OC, C, KH, KW) * scale
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              if self.W.data is None:
                  self.in_channels = inputs.shape[1]
                  self._init_W()
      
              return conv2d(inputs, self.W, self.b, self.stride, self.pad)
      ```
      
      ## 2. 鍒嗙粍鍗风Н (Grouped Convolution)
      
      ```python
      class Conv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0,
                       nobias=False, in_channels=None, groups=1):
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
              self.groups = groups
              assert out_channels % groups == 0
              assert in_channels % groups == 0
      
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
          def _init_W(self):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
              scale = np.sqrt(1 / ((C // self.groups) * KH * KW))
              self.W.data = np.random.randn(OC, C//self.groups, KH, KW) * scale
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
              return grouped_conv2d(inputs, self.W, self.b, self.stride, self.pad, self.groups)
      ```
      
      ## 3. 娣卞害鍙垎绂诲嵎绉?(Depthwise Separable Convolution)
      
      ```python
      class Conv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0,
                       nobias=False, in_channels=None, depthwise=False):
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
              self.depthwise = depthwise
              if depthwise:
                  self.groups = in_channels
                  assert out_channels % in_channels == 0
                  self.channel_multiplier = out_channels // in_channels
      
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
          def _init_W(self):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
              scale = np.sqrt(1 / (C * KH * KW))
              self.W.data = np.random.randn(OC, 1, KH, KW) * scale
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉鍚岀殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
              # 閫愰€氶亾鍗风Н
              y = depthwise_conv2d(inputs, self.W, None, self.stride, self.pad)
      
              # 1x1鍗风Н
              if self.channel_multiplier > 1:
                  C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
                  w = Parameter(np.random.randn(OC, C, 1, 1) * np.sqrt(1/C), name='W_1x1')
                  y = conv2d(y, w, self.b, 1, 0)
              elif self.b is not None:
                  y += self.b.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
      
              return y
      ```

      ## 5. 杞疆鍗风Н (Transposed Convolution)
      
      ```python
      class Deconv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0,
                       nobias=False, in_channels=None):
              # 涓庢櫘閫氬嵎绉浉浼肩殑閮ㄥ垎
              ...
      
          def _init_W(self):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
              scale = np.sqrt(1 / (C * KH * KW))
              self.W.data = np.random.randn(C, OC, KH, KW) * scale
      
          def forward(self, x):
              if self.W.data is None:
                  self.in_channels = x.shape[1]
                  self._init_W()
      
              return deconv2d(x, self.W, self.b, self.stride, self.pad)
      ```
      
      ## 6. 鏍稿績鍗风Н鍑芥暟
      
      ### 6.1 conv2d 鍑芥暟
      
      ```python
      def conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1):
          # 1. 杈撳叆杞垪
          col = im2col_array(x, W.shape[2:], stride, pad, dilation=dilation)
          # 2. 寮犻噺鐐圭Н
          y = np.tensordot(col, W, ((1, 2, 3), (1, 2, 3)))
          # 3. 娣诲姞鍋忕疆
          if b is not None:
              y += b
          # 4. 璋冩暣褰㈢姸
          return np.rollaxis(y, 3, 1)
      ```
      
      ### 6.2 depthwise_conv2d 鍑芥暟
      
      ```python
      def depthwise_conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1):
          N, C, H, W_in = x.shape
          OC, _, KH, KW = W.shape
          y = np.zeros((N, OC,
                       get_conv_outsize(H, KH, stride[0], pad[0], dilation[0]),
                       get_conv_outsize(W_in, KW, stride[1], pad[1], dilation[1])),
                      dtype=x.dtype)
      
          # 閫愰€氶亾鍗风Н
          for i in range(C):
              x_channel = x[:, i:i+1, :, :]
              W_channel = W[i*OC//C:(i+1)*OC//C, :, :, :]
              y[:, i*OC//C:(i+1)*OC//C, :, :] = conv2d(x_channel, W_channel, None,
                                                        stride, pad, dilation)
      
          if b is not None:
              y += b.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
      
          return y
      ```

      ## 1. M1-06锛氬嵎绉?/ 姹犲寲鍙嶅悜 kernel

      鏀归€犲悗锛屼笉鍐嶅垱寤鸿繖鍥涗釜 `2脳2` 灞€閮ㄥ潡銆侴PU 鐩存帴鍚姩 `N脳C脳H脳W=16` 涓嚎绋嬶紱渚嬪璐熻矗 `gx[0,0,1,1]` 鐨勭嚎绋嬩細鍙嶆煡鍒板畠灞炰簬杈撳嚭绐楀彛 `(oh,ow)=(0,0)`锛屽啀妫€鏌?`indexes[0,0,0,0]=3` 鎭板ソ瀵瑰簲绐楀彛鍙充笅浣嶇疆锛屼簬鏄妸 `gy[0,0,0,0]=a` 鍐欏叆杩欎釜 `gx` 浣嶇疆銆傛瘡涓嚎绋嬮兘鍋氬悓鏍风殑鍙嶆煡锛屾渶缁堝緱鍒板畬鍏ㄧ浉鍚岀殑 `gx`銆?
      褰?stride 灏忎簬绐楀彛灏哄鏃讹紝绐楀彛浼氶噸鍙犮€傛敼閫犲墠鐢?`col2im` 鐨勫娆?`+=` 瀹屾垚閲嶅彔鍖哄煙姹傚拰锛涙敼閫犲悗鐢扁€滆杈撳叆鍍忕礌瀵瑰簲鐨勪竴涓嚎绋嬧€濊鍙栨墍鏈夎鐩栧畠鐨?`gy` 骞跺湪瀵勫瓨鍣ㄤ腑姹傚拰銆傛暟瀛︾粨鏋滅浉鍚岋紝鍙槸姹傚拰鐨勪綅缃粠澶т腑闂存暟缁勮浆绉诲埌浜嗗崟涓嚎绋嬪唴閮ㄣ€?
      > 鍗风Н `gx` 鐨勫舰鐘跺彉鍖栦笌 MaxPool 寰堝儚锛屼絾 `gcol` 鐨勫唴瀹逛笉鍚岋細MaxPool 鐨勬瘡涓?`KH脳KW` 灞€閮ㄥ潡鍙湁 argmax 浣嶇疆闈為浂锛涘嵎绉殑 `gcol` 鍏堢敱 `W` 鍜?`gy` 鐩镐箻寰楀埌锛屽眬閮ㄥ潡涓殑澶氫釜浣嶇疆閮藉彲鑳芥湁鍊笺€傞閫?gather 鍗风Н kernel 鍚屾牱璺宠繃 `gcol`锛岀洿鎺ヨ涓€涓嚎绋嬭绠椾竴涓?`gx[n,c,h,w]`锛屽湪鍐呴儴绱姞鎵€鏈夋湁鏁堢殑 `gy脳W` 椤广€?
      ### 1.2 鏈€鎺ㄨ崘鐨勬敼鍐欐柟妗堬細gather 鍨?col2im
      
      鈥済ather鈥濆彲浠ョ洿璇戜负鈥滀富鍔ㄥ幓璇诲彇鈥濄€傛敼閫犲悗涓嶅啀璁╄緭鍑烘搴﹀厛鍐欏叆涓€涓ぇ `gcol`锛岃€屾槸璁╂瘡涓?GPU 绾跨▼鐩存帴璐熻矗涓€涓緭鍏ュ儚绱狅細
      
      1. 绾跨▼鍏堢‘瀹氳嚜宸辫礋璐ｇ殑杈撳叆浣嶇疆 `(n, c, h, w)`锛?2. 鍙嶅悜妫€鏌ヨ繖涓儚绱犺鍝簺姹犲寲绐楀彛鎴栧嵎绉獥鍙ｈ鐩栵紱
      3. 鐩存帴浠?`gy` 璇诲彇瀵瑰簲姊害锛岀疮鍔犲悗鍐欏叆 `gx[n,c,h,w]`銆?
      鎵€浠ワ紝棣栭€夋柟妗堜腑鐨?CUDA C 绠楀瓙骞朵笉鏄€滆涓嶅悓绾跨▼鍒嗗埆绠?`gcol` 鐨勪笉鍚岀墖娈碘€濄€傛伆鎭扮浉鍙嶏紝瀹冪殑鐩爣鏄烦杩?`gcol`锛氫笉鍚岀嚎绋嬪垎鍒绠椾笉鍚岀殑 `gx` 杈撳叆浣嶇疆锛屾瘡涓嚎绋嬭嚜宸卞弽鏌ョ浉鍏崇獥鍙ｅ苟瀹屾垚绱姞銆傝嫢閲囩敤鍙︿竴绉?scatter 鏂规锛屾墠浼氭洿鎺ヨ繎鈥滀竴涓嚎绋嬪鐞嗕竴涓緭鍑虹獥鍙ｏ紝鍐嶆妸缁撴灉鍐欏叆 gcol 鎴?gx鈥濓紱杩欐椂澶氫釜绾跨▼鍙兘鍐欏悓涓€浣嶇疆锛岄渶瑕?`atomicAdd`锛岄€熷害鍜岀‘瀹氭€ч兘浼氬彈鍒板奖鍝嶃€?
      杩欎釜鏂规鐨勪紭鐐规槸锛?
      - 涓嶅垱寤?`gcol`锛屾樉钁楅檷浣庢樉瀛樺嘲鍊硷紱
      - 姣忎釜绾跨▼鍙啓涓€涓?`gx` 浣嶇疆锛屼笉闇€瑕佸涓嚎绋嬫姠鍐欏悓涓€涓湴鍧€锛?- 涓嶄娇鐢?`atomicAdd`锛屼袱娆¤繍琛屽彲浠ヤ繚鎸佷竴鑷达紝渚夸簬娴嬭瘯锛?- 鍚屼竴涓€濊矾鍙互澶嶇敤浜?MaxPool 鍙嶅悜銆佸嵎绉?`gx` 鍜?dilation 鍒嗘敮銆?
      绗竴鐗堝缓璁彧鏀光€滄暟鎹惉杩愬拰绐楀彛绱姞鈥濓紝鐭╅樀涔樻硶浠嶄氦缁?CuPy/cuBLAS銆俢uBLAS 鏄?NVIDIA 宸茬粡浼樺寲濂界殑鐭╅樀涔樻硶搴擄紝鎴戜滑娌℃湁蹇呰鍦ㄧ涓€鐗堥噸鏂板啓 GEMM銆?
      ### 1.3 鏀瑰啓绀轰緥锛歁axPool 鍙嶅悜
      
      #### 鏀归€犲墠锛歅ython/CuPy 鐗堟湰
      
      涓嬮潰鏄綋鍓嶉€昏緫鐨勭畝鍖栫増銆傚畠鐨勯噸鐐规槸鍏堝垱寤?`gcol`锛屽啀璋冪敤 `col2im`锛?
      ```python
      def maxpool_backward_before(gy, indexes, input_shape,
                                  kernel_size, stride, pad):
          N, C, OH, OW = gy.shape
          N, C, H, W = input_shape
          KH, KW = kernel_size
          xp = get_array_module(gy)
      
          # 绗竴姝ワ細鍒涘缓 KH*KW 鍊嶅ぇ灏忕殑涓存椂鏁扮粍
          gcol = xp.zeros(N * C * OH * OW * KH * KW,
                          dtype=gy.dtype)
      
          # 绗簩姝ワ細鎸?argmax 鎶?gy 鏁ｅ啓杩涘幓
          offsets = (indexes.ravel()
                     + xp.arange(0, indexes.size * KH * KW,
                                 KH * KW))
          gcol[offsets] = gy.ravel()
      
          # 绗笁姝ワ細鏁寸悊缁村害锛屽啀閫氳繃 Python 寰幆鎵ц col2im
          gcol = gcol.reshape(N, C, OH, OW, KH, KW)
          gcol = xp.swapaxes(gcol, 2, 4)
          gcol = xp.swapaxes(gcol, 3, 5)
          return col2im_array(gcol, (N, C, H, W),
                              kernel_size, stride, pad,
                              to_matrix=False)
      ```
      
      杩欐浠ｇ爜鍔熻兘姝ｇ‘锛屼絾涓€娆″弽鍚戝寘鍚€滃垱寤轰复鏃舵暟缁勩€佹暎鍐欍€佹崲缁村害銆佸涓?col2im kernel鈥濆嚑姝ャ€?
      #### 鏀归€犲悗锛氫竴涓?CUDA kernel 鐩存帴寰楀埌 gx
      
      涓嬮潰鐨勪唬鐮佷粛鐒剁敱 Python 璋冪敤锛屼絾鐪熸鐨勭獥鍙ｉ亶鍘嗗湪 GPU kernel 鍐呭畬鎴愩€備负浜嗕究浜庨槄璇伙紝绀轰緥浣跨敤 `float` 鍜?NCHW 甯冨眬锛涘疄闄呮帴鍏ユ椂鍐嶈ˉ鍏?`float16/float64` 鍒嗘敮鍜?dtype 妫€鏌ャ€?
      ```python
      import cupy as cp
      
      maxpool_bwd_kernel = cp.RawKernel(r'''
      extern "C" __global__ void maxpool_bwd(
          const long long* indexes, const float* gy, float* gx,
          int N, int C, int H, int W,

## Activity

    $ "C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "Get-Content code\\eneuro\\nn\\module.py | Select-Object -First 430"
    status: Completed · exit 0
      import os
      import weakref
      import numpy as np
      try:
          import cupy as cp
          has_cupy = True
      except ImportError:
          has_cupy = False
      from ..base import functions as F
      from ..base import *
      from ..base.parameter import Parameter
      from ..base.functions import pair, get_array_module
      from ..base.functions import depthwise_conv2d, grouped_conv2d
      from ..utils.statedict import StateDict
      
      from ..global_config import VISUAL_CONFIG
      import cv2
      
      #function涓烘柟娉曪紝閫氱敤宸ュ叿鑸嶅純淇濆瓨鍙傛暟鍔熻兘锛宭ayer涓哄眰锛屼繚瀛樺弬鏁板姛鑳斤紝涔熷叿澶囧眰灞傚祵濂楀姛鑳?
      class Layer:
          def __init__(self, device='cpu'):
              self.device = device
              self._params = set()
          #閲嶅啓setattr鏂规硶锛屽綋璁剧疆鐨勫睘鎬ф槸Parameter鎴朙ayer鏃讹紝灏嗗睘鎬у悕鍔犲叆_params闆嗗悎锛岃嚜鍔ㄨ瘑鍒弬鏁板拰瀛愬眰
          def __setattr__(self, name, value):
              if isinstance(value, (Parameter, Layer)):
                  self._params.add(name)
              super().__setattr__(name, value)
      
          def __call__(self, *inputs):
              outputs = self.forward(*inputs)
              if not isinstance(outputs, tuple):
                  outputs = (outputs,)
              self.inputs = [weakref.ref(x) for x in inputs]
              self.outputs = [weakref.ref(y) for y in outputs]
      
              if len(outputs) == 0:
                  raise
              return outputs if len(outputs) > 1 else outputs[0]
      
          def to(self, device='cpu'):
              if device == self.device:
                  return self
      
              for name in self._params:
                  obj = self.__dict__[name]
                  if isinstance(obj, Layer):
                      obj.to(device)
                  else:
                      param = obj
                      if param.data is None:
                          param.device = device
                      elif device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                          if has_cupy and isinstance(param.data, np.ndarray):
                              param._data = cp.asarray(param.data)
                          param.device = 'cuda'
                      else:  # cpu
                          if has_cupy and isinstance(param.data, cp.ndarray):
                              param._data = cp.asnumpy(param.data)
                          param.device = 'cpu'
      
              self.device = device
              return self
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              raise NotImplementedError()
      
          #鍙傛暟鐢熸垚鍣紝閫掑綊鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夊弬鏁?
          def params(self):
              for name in self._params:
                  obj = self.__dict__[name]
      
                  if isinstance(obj, Layer):
                      yield from obj.params()
                  else:
                      yield obj
      
          def cleargrads(self):
              for param in self.params():
                  param.cleargrad()
      
          def get_params_list(self) -> list['Parameter']:
              """
              鏀堕泦骞惰繑鍥炲綋鍓嶅眰鍙婂叾鎵€鏈夊瓙灞備腑鐨勬墍鏈夊彲璁粌鍙傛暟銆?
      
              Returns:
                  list[Parameter]: 涓€涓寘鍚墍鏈?Parameter 瀵硅薄鐨勫垪琛ㄣ€?
              """
              params_dict = {}
              self._flatten_params(params_dict)
              # 浠庡瓧鍏哥殑鍊间腑鍒涘缓涓€涓垪琛ㄥ苟杩斿洖
              return list(params_dict.values())
      
          #涓轰繚瀛樺弬鏁版潵璁板綍鍙傛暟瀛楀吀
          def _flatten_params(self, params_dict, parent_key=""):
              for name in self._params:
                  obj = self.__dict__[name]
                  key = parent_key + '/' + name if parent_key else name
      
                  if isinstance(obj, Layer):
                      obj._flatten_params(params_dict, key)
                  else:
                      params_dict[key] = obj
      
      #鍦╢unction绫讳腑宸茶缃父鐢ㄧ殑鍑芥暟鏀寔鍙嶅悜浼犳挱锛屽悗缁璴ayer鍙渶瑕佸墠鍚戜紶鎾嚜鍔ㄥ舰鎴愯绠楀浘锛屾敮鎸佸弽鍚戜紶鎾?
      #layer绫讳腑宸茶缃弬鏁扮鐞嗗姛鑳斤紝鍚庣画layer鍙渶瑕佸畾涔夊弬鏁板嵆鍙嚜鍔ㄦ敮鎸佸弬鏁扮鐞?
      
      class Linear(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_size, nobias=False, dtype='float32', in_size=None):
              super().__init__()
              self.in_size = in_size
              self.out_size = out_size
              self.dtype = dtype
      
              self.W = Parameter(None, name='W')
              if self.in_size is not None:
                  xp = np
                  if has_cupy and self.device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                      xp = cp
                  self._init_W(xp)
      
              if nobias:
                  self.b = None
              else:
                  self.b = Parameter(np.zeros(out_size, dtype=dtype), name='b')
      
          def _init_params_on_device(self):
              """鍒濆鍖栨椂鏍规嵁璁惧绫诲瀷鍒涘缓鍙傛暟"""
              xp = np
              if has_cupy and self.device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                  xp = cp
      
              if self.b is not None:
                  self.b.data = xp.array(self.b.data)
      
              if self.W.data is not None:
                  self.W.data = xp.array(self.W.data)
      
          def _init_W(self, xp):
              I, O = self.in_size, self.out_size
              W_data = xp.random.randn(I, O).astype(self.dtype) * xp.sqrt(1 / I)
              self.W.data = W_data
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              inputs = inputs.to(self.device)
      
              if self.W.data is None:
                  self.in_size = inputs.shape[1]
                  self._init_W(get_array_module(inputs.data))
      
              y = linear(inputs, self.W, self.b)
              return y
      
      class Conv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1,
                       pad=0, nobias=False, dtype='float32', in_channels=None, visualize=False,
                       groups=1, depthwise=False, dilation=1):
              super().__init__()
              self.in_channels = in_channels
              self.out_channels = out_channels
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
              self.dtype = dtype
              self.visualize = visualize
              self.groups = groups
              self.depthwise = depthwise
              self.dilation = dilation
      
              # 娣卞害鍙垎绂诲嵎绉殑鐗规畩澶勭悊
              if self.depthwise:
                  if self.in_channels is None:
                      raise ValueError("depthwise convolution requires in_channels to be specified")
                  self.groups = self.in_channels
                  assert out_channels % in_channels == 0, "out_channels must be divisible by in_channels for depthwise convolution"
                  self.channel_multiplier = out_channels // in_channels
              else:
                  assert out_channels % groups == 0, "out_channels must be divisible by groups"
                  if in_channels is not None:
                      assert in_channels % groups == 0, "in_channels must be divisible by groups"
      
              self.W = Parameter(None, name='W')
              if in_channels is not None:
                  xp = np
                  if has_cupy and self.device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                      xp = cp
                  self._init_W(xp)
      
              if nobias:
                  self.b = None
              else:
                  self.b = Parameter(np.zeros(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='b')
      
          def _init_W(self, xp):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
      
              if self.depthwise:
                  # 娣卞害鍙垎绂诲嵎绉殑鏉冮噸褰㈢姸: (OC, 1, KH, KW)
                  scale = xp.sqrt(1 / (C * KH * KW))
                  W_data = xp.random.randn(OC, 1, KH, KW).astype(self.dtype) * scale
              else:
                  # 鏅€氬嵎绉垨鍒嗙粍鍗风Н鐨勬潈閲嶅舰鐘? (OC, C//groups, KH, KW)
                  scale = xp.sqrt(1 / ((C // self.groups) * KH * KW))
                  W_data = xp.random.randn(OC, C // self.groups, KH, KW).astype(self.dtype) * scale
      
              self.W.data = W_data
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              inputs = inputs.to(self.device)
      
              if self.W.data is None:
                  self.in_channels = inputs.shape[1]
                  self._init_W(get_array_module(inputs.data))
      
              if self.depthwise:
                  # 娣卞害鍙垎绂诲嵎绉? 鍏堣繘琛岄€愰€氶亾鍗风Н锛屽啀杩涜1x1鍗风Н
                  # 閫愰€氶亾鍗风Н
                  y = depthwise_conv2d(
                      inputs, self.W, None,
                      stride=self.stride,
                      pad=self.pad,
                      dilation=self.dilation,
                      visualize=self.visualize
                  )
                  # 1x1鍗风Н
                  if self.channel_multiplier > 1:
                      xp = np
                      if has_cupy and self.device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                          xp = cp
                      # 鍒涘缓1x1鍗风Н鏍?
                      C = self.in_channels
                      OC = self.out_channels
                      w_shape = (OC, C, 1, 1)
                      scale = xp.sqrt(1 / C)
                      w_data = xp.random.randn(*w_shape).astype(self.dtype) * scale
                      w = Parameter(w_data, name='W_1x1')
                      y = conv2d(y, w, self.b, stride=1, pad=0, visualize=self.visualize)
                  else:
                      if self.b is not None:
                          y += self.b.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
              elif self.groups > 1:
                  # 鍒嗙粍鍗风Н
                  y = grouped_conv2d(
                      inputs, self.W, self.b,
                      stride=self.stride,
                      pad=self.pad,
                      groups=self.groups,
                      dilation=self.dilation,
                      visualize=self.visualize
                  )
              else:
                  # 鏅€氬嵎绉?
                  y = conv2d(
                      inputs, self.W, self.b,
                      stride=self.stride,
                      pad=self.pad,
                      dilation=self.dilation,
                      visualize=self.visualize
                  )
              return y

      class Deconv2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1,
                       pad=0, dilation=1, nobias=False, dtype='float32', in_channels=None, visualize=False):
      
              super().__init__()
              self.in_channels = in_channels
              self.out_channels = out_channels
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
              self.dilation = dilation
              self.dtype = dtype
              self.visualize = visualize
      
              self.W = Parameter(None, name='W')
              if in_channels is not None:
                  xp = np
                  if has_cupy and self.device in ['cuda', 'gpu']:
                      xp = cp
                  self._init_W(xp)
      
              if nobias:
                  self.b = None
              else:
                  self.b = Parameter(np.zeros(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='b')
      
          def _init_W(self, xp):
              C, OC = self.in_channels, self.out_channels
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
              scale = xp.sqrt(1 / (C * KH * KW))
              W_data = xp.random.randn(C, OC, KH, KW).astype(self.dtype) * scale
              self.W.data = W_data
      
          def forward(self, x):
              x = x.to(self.device)
      
              if self.W.data is None:
                  self.in_channels = x.shape[1]
                  self._init_W(get_array_module(x.data))
      
              y = deconv2d(x, self.W, self.b, self.stride, self.pad, dilation=self.dilation, visualize=self.visualize)
              return y

      class BatchNorm(Layer):
          def __init__(self, num_features, num_dims=4, eps=1e-5, momentum=0.9, dtype='float32'):
              super().__init__()
              self.num_features = num_features
              self.num_dims = num_dims
              self.eps = eps
              self.momentum = momentum
              self.dtype = dtype
      
              # 涓哄吋瀹?BatchNorm2d 鍚庣锛実amma/beta 缁熶竴浣跨敤 (C,) 褰㈢姸
              self.gamma = Parameter(np.ones(num_features, dtype=dtype), name='gamma')
              self.beta = Parameter(np.zeros(num_features, dtype=dtype), name='beta')
      
              # 杩愯缁熻閲忎互 Parameter 褰㈠紡淇濆瓨锛堜笉鍙備笌姊害锛?
              self.running_mean = Parameter(np.zeros(num_features, dtype=dtype), name='running_mean')
              self.running_mean.requires_grad = False
              self.running_var = Parameter(np.ones(num_features, dtype=dtype), name='running_var')
              self.running_var.requires_grad = False
      
              # 鍏煎鏃у睘鎬у悕
              self.moving_mean = self.running_mean.data
              self.moving_var = self.running_var.data
      
          def forward(self, x):
              x = x.to(self.device)
      
              if self.num_dims == 2:
                  n, c = x.shape
                  x_4d = x.reshape(n, c, 1, 1)
                  y_4d = batch_norm2d((x_4d, self.gamma, self.beta), self.running_mean, self.running_var, self.momentum, self.eps)
                  y = y_4d.reshape(n, c)
              else:
                  y = batch_norm2d((x, self.gamma, self.beta), self.running_mean, self.running_var, self.momentum, self.eps)
      
              # 淇濇寔鏃у瓧娈靛悓姝?
              self.moving_mean = self.running_mean.data
              self.moving_var = self.running_var.data
              return y
      
      class BatchNorm2d(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, momentum=0.9, eps=1e-5, dtype='float32'):
              super().__init__()
              self.out_channels = out_channels
              self.momentum = momentum
              self.eps = eps
      
              # 鍙缁冨弬鏁?纬 鍜?尾
              self.gamma = Parameter(np.ones(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='gamma')
              self.beta  = Parameter(np.zeros(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='beta')
              # 涓嶅彲璁粌鐨勮繍琛岀粺璁￠噺锛堜粛鐢?Tensor 瀛樺偍锛屼絾 requires_grad=False锛?
              self.running_mean = Parameter(np.zeros(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='running_mean')
              self.running_mean.requires_grad=False
              self.running_var  = Parameter(np.ones(out_channels, dtype=dtype), name='running_var')
              self.running_var.requires_grad=False
      
          def forward(self, inputs):
              inputs = inputs.to(self.device)
      
              x = (inputs, self.gamma, self.beta)
              y = batch_norm2d(x, self.running_mean, self.running_var, self.momentum, self.eps)
              return y
      
      class FusedConvReLU(Layer):
          def __init__(self, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0,
                       nobias=False, dtype='float32', in_channels=None, visualize=False,
                       groups=1, depthwise=False, dilation=1):

              self.conv = Conv2d(out_channels, kernel_size, stride, pad,
                       nobias, dtype, in_channels, visualize,
                       groups, depthwise, dilation)
      
          def forward(self, inputs):

                  # 娣卞害鍙垎绂诲嵎绉? 鍏堣繘琛岄€愰€氶亾鍗风Н锛屽啀杩涜1x1鍗风Н
                  print("Warning: Not supported fusion (depthwise conv). Using not fused function.")

                  y = relu(y)

                  # 鍒嗙粍鍗风Н

                  y = relu(y)

                  # 鏅€氬嵎绉?
                  y = fused_conv_relu(inputs, self.conv.W, self.conv.b, self.conv.stride,
                                      self.conv.pad, self.conv.dilation, self.conv.visualize)
              return y

      def conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1, visualize=False):
          return Conv2d(stride, pad, dilation, visualize)(x, W, b)
      
      
      def conv2d_backward_input_array(gy, W, stride=(1, 1), pad=(0, 0), dilation=(1, 1), out_h=None, out_w=None):
          """璁＄畻鍗风Н瀵硅緭鍏?x 鐨勬搴︼紙鏀寔 dilation锛夈€?
      
          Args:
              gy: 涓婃父姊害锛屽舰鐘?(N, OC, OH, OW)
              W: 鍗风Н鏍革紝褰㈢姸 (OC, C, KH, KW)
          """
          SH, SW = pair(stride)
          PH, PW = pair(pad)
          DH, DW = pair(dilation)
      
          N, OC, OH, OW = gy.shape
          OC_W, C, KH, KW = W.shape
          assert OC == OC_W
      
          if out_h is None or out_w is None:
              out_h = SH * (OH - 1) - 2 * PH + DH * (KH - 1) + 1
              out_w = SW * (OW - 1) - 2 * PW + DW * (KW - 1) + 1
      
          xp = get_array_module(gy)
      
          gx_pad = xp.zeros((N, C, out_h + 2 * PH + SH - 1, out_w + 2 * PW + SW - 1), dtype=gy.dtype)
      
          for kh in range(KH):
              h_start = kh * DH
              h_end = h_start + SH * OH
              for kw in range(KW):
                  w_start = kw * DW
                  w_end = w_start + SW * OW
      
                  # (N, OC, OH, OW) x (OC, C) -> (N, OH, OW, C) -> (N, C, OH, OW)
                  contrib = xp.tensordot(gy, W[:, :, kh, kw], axes=(1, 0)).transpose(0, 3, 1, 2)
                  gx_pad[:, :, h_start:h_end:SH, w_start:w_end:SW] += contrib
      
          return gx_pad[:, :, PH:PH + out_h, PW:PW + out_w]
      
      
      class GroupedConv2d(Function):
          def __init__(self, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), groups=1, dilation=1, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.stride = pair(stride)
              self.pad = pair(pad)
              self.groups = groups
              self.dilation = pair(dilation)
              self.visualize = visualize
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              """
              鍒嗙粍鍗风Н鐨勫墠鍚戜紶鎾€?
      
              Args:
                  x: 杈撳叆鏁版嵁锛屽舰鐘?(N, C, H, W)
                  W: 鏉冮噸锛屽舰鐘?(OC, C_per_group, KH, KW)
                  b: 鍋忕疆锛屽彲涓篘one
      
              Returns:
                  杈撳嚭鏁版嵁
      
              GPU閫傞厤璇存槑:
                  - 浼樺厛浠庢潈閲峎鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡绫诲瀷
                  - 褰揥鏄痗upy鏁扮粍鏃讹紝闇€瑕佸厛灏唜杞崲涓篶upy鏁扮粍
              """
              x = xs[0]
              W = xs[1]
              b = xs[2]
      
              N, C, H, W_in = x.shape
              OC, C_per_group, KH, KW = W.shape
      
              assert C % self.groups == 0, "Input channels must be divisible by groups"
              assert OC % self.groups == 0, "Output channels must be divisible by groups"
      
              # 鍏抽敭淇锛氫紭鍏堜粠鏉冮噸W鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡锛岃€岄潪杈撳叆x
              W_data = W.data if isinstance(W, Tensor) else W
              xp = get_array_module(W_data)
      
              # 濡傛灉x涓嶆槸xp绫诲瀷鐨勬暟缁勶紝闇€瑕佸厛杞崲
              # 浣跨敤to_xp杈呭姪鍑芥暟姝ｇ‘澶勭悊numpy/cupy涔嬮棿鐨勮浆鎹?
              x_data = to_xp(x, xp)
      
              OC_per_group = OC // self.groups
              OH = get_conv_outsize(H, KH, self.stride[0], self.pad[0], self.dilation[0])
              OW = get_conv_outsize(W_in, KW, self.stride[1], self.pad[1], self.dilation[1])
              y = xp.zeros((N, OC, OH, OW), dtype=x_data.dtype)
      
              for i in range(self.groups):
                  x_group = x_data[:, i*C_per_group:(i+1)*C_per_group, :, :]
                  W_group = W_data[i*OC_per_group:(i+1)*OC_per_group, :, :, :]
      
                  col = im2col_array(
                      x_group, (KH, KW), self.stride, self.pad,
                      to_matrix=False, dilation=self.dilation, xp=xp
                  )
                  y_group = xp.tensordot(col, W_group, ((1, 2, 3), (1, 2, 3)))
                  y_group = xp.rollaxis(y_group, 3, 1)
                  y[:, i*OC_per_group:(i+1)*OC_per_group, :, :] = y_group
      
              if b is not None:
                  b_data = to_xp(b, xp)
                  y += b_data.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
      
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              x, W, b = self.inputs
              x_data = x.data
              W_data = W.data
              gy = gys.data
      
              N, C, H, W_in = x_data.shape
              OC, C_per_group, KH, KW = W_data.shape
              OC_per_group = OC // self.groups
      
              xp = get_array_module(gy)
      
              gx = xp.zeros_like(x_data)
              gW = xp.zeros_like(W_data)
      
              for i in range(self.groups):
                  c0, c1 = i * C_per_group, (i + 1) * C_per_group
                  oc0, oc1 = i * OC_per_group, (i + 1) * OC_per_group
      
                  x_group = x_data[:, c0:c1, :, :]
                  gy_group = gy[:, oc0:oc1, :, :]
                  W_group = W_data[oc0:oc1, :, :, :]
      
                  gx_group = conv2d_backward_input_array(
                      gy_group,
                      W_group,
                      stride=self.stride,
                      pad=self.pad,
                      dilation=self.dilation,
                      out_h=H,
                      out_w=W_in,
                  )
                  gx[:, c0:c1, :, :] += gx_group
      
                  col = im2col_array(
                      x_group, (KH, KW), self.stride, self.pad,
                      to_matrix=False, dilation=self.dilation
                  )
                  gW_group = xp.tensordot(gy_group, col, ((0, 2, 3), (0, 4, 5)))
                  gW[oc0:oc1, :, :, :] = gW_group
      
              gb = None
              if b.data is not None:
                  gb = gy.sum(axis=(0, 2, 3))
      
              return as_Tensor(gx), as_Tensor(gW), (as_Tensor(gb) if gb is not None else None)
      
      
      def grouped_conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), groups=1, dilation=1, visualize=False):
          return GroupedConv2d(stride, pad, groups, dilation, visualize)(x, W, b)
      
      
      # 娣卞害鍗风Н锛堟湰璐ㄤ笂鏄?groups == in_channels 鐨勫垎缁勫嵎绉級
      def depthwise_conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1, visualize=False):
          x = as_Tensor(x)
          groups = x.shape[1]
          return grouped_conv2d(x, W, b, stride=stride, pad=pad, groups=groups, dilation=dilation, visualize=visualize)
      
      
      class Deconv2d(Function):
          def __init__(self, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), outsize=None, dilation=1, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.stride = pair(stride)
              self.pad = pair(pad)
              self.outsize = outsize
              self.dilation = pair(dilation)
              self.visualize = visualize
      
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              """
              杞疆鍗风Н鐨勫墠鍚戜紶鎾€?
      
              Args:
                  x: 杈撳叆鏁版嵁锛屽舰鐘?(N, C, H, W)
                  W: 鏉冮噸锛屽舰鐘?(C, OC, KH, KW)
                  b: 鍋忕疆锛屽彲涓篘one
      
              Returns:
                  杈撳嚭鏁版嵁
      
              GPU閫傞厤璇存槑:
                  - 浼樺厛浠庢潈閲峎鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡绫诲瀷
                  - 褰揥鏄痗upy鏁扮粍鏃讹紝闇€瑕佸厛灏唜杞崲涓篶upy鏁扮粍
              """
              x = xs[0]
              W = xs[1]
              b = xs[2]
              Weight = W
              SH, SW = self.stride
              PH, PW = self.pad
              DH, DW = self.dilation
              C, OC, KH, KW = Weight.shape
              N, C, H, W_in = x.shape
              if self.outsize is None:
                  out_h = get_deconv_outsize(H, KH, SH, PH)
                  out_w = get_deconv_outsize(W_in, KW, SW, PW)
              else:
                  out_h, out_w = pair(self.outsize)
              img_shape = (N, OC, out_h, out_w)
      
              # 鍏抽敭淇锛氫紭鍏堜粠鏉冮噸W鑾峰彇鏁扮粍妯″潡锛岃€岄潪杈撳叆x
              W_data = W.data if isinstance(W, Tensor) else W
              xp = get_array_module(W_data)
      
              # 濡傛灉x涓嶆槸xp绫诲瀷鐨勬暟缁勶紝闇€瑕佸厛杞崲
              # 浣跨敤to_xp杈呭姪鍑芥暟姝ｇ‘澶勭悊numpy/cupy涔嬮棿鐨勮浆鎹?
              x_data = to_xp(x, xp)
      
              gcol = xp.tensordot(W_data, x_data, (0, 1))
              gcol = xp.rollaxis(gcol, 3)
              y = col2im_array(gcol, img_shape, (KH, KW), self.stride, self.pad,
                               to_matrix=False)
              if b is not None:
                  self.no_bias = True
                  b_data = to_xp(b, xp)
                  y += b_data.reshape((1, b_data.size, 1, 1))
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              x, W, b = self.inputs
      
              # ==== gx ====
              gx = conv2d(gys, W, b=None, stride=self.stride, pad=self.pad, dilation=self.dilation)
              # ==== gW ====
              gW = Conv2DGradW(self)(gys, x)
              # ==== gb ====
              gb = None
              if b.data is not None:
                  gb = gys.sum(axis=(0, 2, 3))
              return gx, gW, gb
      
      
      def deconv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), outsize=None, dilation=1, visualize=False):
          return Deconv2d(stride, pad, outsize, dilation, visualize)(x, W, b)

          #     ggy = conv2d(x, gW, stride=self.stride, pad=self.pad)
          #     return gx, ggy
      
      #鏀瑰彉缁村害锛屾睜鍖栨搷浣滄壘鍒癒H * KW鏈€澶у€肩殑浣嶇疆
      class Pooling(Function):
          def __init__(self, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
              self.visualize = visualize
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              col = im2col_array(x, self.kernel_size, self.stride, self.pad,
                                 to_matrix=False)
      
              N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW = col.shape
              col = col.reshape(N, C, KH * KW, OH, OW)
              self.indexes = col.argmax(axis=2)
              y = col.max(axis=2)
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              return Pooling2DGrad(self)(gys)
      
      
      class Pooling2DGrad(Function):
          def __init__(self, mpool2d,visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.visualize = visualize
              self.mpool2d = mpool2d
              self.kernel_size = mpool2d.kernel_size
              self.stride = mpool2d.stride
              self.pad = mpool2d.pad
              self.input_shape = mpool2d.inputs[0].shape
              self.dtype = mpool2d.inputs[0].dtype
              self.indexes = mpool2d.indexes
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              gy = xs[0]
      
      
              N, C, OH, OW = gy.shape
              N, C, H, W = self.input_shape
              KH, KW = pair(self.kernel_size)
      
              xp = get_array_module(gy)
      
              #涓庢睜鍖栨搷浣滅殑缁村害鐩稿悓
              gcol = xp.zeros((N * C * OH * OW * KH * KW), dtype=self.dtype)
      
              indexes = (self.indexes.ravel()
                         + xp.arange(0, self.indexes.size * KH * KW, KH * KW))
      
              gcol[indexes] = gy.ravel()
              gcol = gcol.reshape(N, C, OH, OW, KH, KW)
              #涔熷彲浠ョ敤transpose
              gcol = xp.swapaxes(gcol, 2, 4)
              gcol = xp.swapaxes(gcol, 3, 5)
      
              gx = col2im_array(gcol, (N, C, H, W), self.kernel_size, self.stride,
                                self.pad, to_matrix=False)
              return gx
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              f = Pooling2DWithIndexes(self.mpool2d)
              return f(gys)
      
      
      class Pooling2DWithIndexes(Function):
          def __init__(self, mpool2d,visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.visualize = visualize
              self.kernel_size = mpool2d.kernel_size
              self.stride = mpool2d.stride
              self.pad = mpool2d.pad
              self.input_shpae = mpool2d.inputs[0].shape
              self.dtype = mpool2d.inputs[0].dtype
              self.indexes = mpool2d.indexes
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              col = im2col_array(x, self.kernel_size, self.stride, self.pad,
                                 to_matrix=False)
              N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW = col.shape
              col = col.reshape(N, C, KH * KW, OH, OW)
              col = col.transpose(0, 1, 3, 4, 2).reshape(-1, KH * KW)
              indexes = self.indexes.ravel()
              col = col[np.arange(len(indexes)), indexes]
              return col.reshape(N, C, OH, OW)
      
      
      def pooling(x, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0, visualize=False):
          return Pooling(kernel_size, stride, pad, visualize)(x)
      
      
      class AveragePooling(Function):
          def __init__(self, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
              self.input_shape = None
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              self.input_shape = x.shape
              col = im2col_array(x, self.kernel_size, self.stride, self.pad,
                                 to_matrix=False)
              y = col.mean(axis=(2, 3))
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              N, C, OH, OW = gys.shape
              KW, KH = pair(self.kernel_size)
              gys /= (KW*KH)
              gcol = broadcast_to(gys.reshape(-1), (KH, KW, N*C*OH*OW))
              gcol = gcol.reshape(KH, KW, N, C, OH, OW).transpose(2, 3, 0, 1, 4, 5)
              gx = col2im(gcol, self.input_shape, self.kernel_size, self.stride,
                          self.pad, to_matrix=False)
              return gx
      
      
      def average_pooling(x, kernel_size, stride=1, pad=0):
          return AveragePooling(kernel_size, stride, pad)(x)
      
      class GlobalAveragePooling(Function):
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              self.input_shape = x.shape
              y = x.mean(axis=(2, 3), keepdims=True)
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gy):
              # 鍏ㄥ眬骞冲潎姹犲寲鐨勫弽鍚戜紶鎾槸灏嗘搴﹀箍鎾洖鍘熷杈撳叆褰㈢姸
              gx = broadcast_to(gy, self.input_shape)
              return gx
      
      def global_average_pooling(x):
          return GlobalAveragePooling()(x)
      
      class BatchNormFunction(Function):
          # 娉ㄦ剰锛氳绫诲凡琚純鐢紝璇蜂娇鐢?BatchNorm2d 鏇夸唬
          def __init__(self, eps=1e-5, momentum=0.9, training=True, moving_mean=None, moving_var=None):
              super().__init__()
              self.eps = eps

      from . import Dataset
      import random
      from typing import Tuple, List
      from ..base import Tensor
      import multiprocessing as mp
      from multiprocessing import Queue, Process
      import numpy as np
      
      
      def _worker_loop(dataset, batch_indices_list, batch_size, drop_last, output_queue):
          """
          瀛愯繘绋嬩腑鐨勬暟鎹姞杞藉惊鐜嚱鏁?
      
          """
          cursor = 0
          total_indices=len(batch_indices_list)
      
      
          while cursor < total_indices:
              remaining = total_indices - cursor
              current_batch_size = min(batch_size, remaining)
      
              if current_batch_size < batch_size and drop_last:
                  break
      
              batch_indices = batch_indices_list[cursor:cursor + current_batch_size]
              cursor += current_batch_size
      
              batch_data = []
              batch_target = []
      
              for idx in batch_indices:
                  data, target = dataset[idx]
                  batch_data.append(data)
                  batch_target.append(target)
      
              batch_data_tensor = Tensor.stack(batch_data)
              batch_target_tensor = Tensor.stack(batch_target)
      
              output_queue.put((batch_data_tensor, batch_target_tensor))
      
          output_queue.put(None)
      
      
      
      class _SyncLoaderIter:
          """
          鍚屾鍔犺浇杩唬鍣紝褰搉um_workers=0鏃朵娇鐢?
      
          鍦ㄤ富杩涚▼涓『搴忓姞杞芥暟鎹紝涓嶆秹鍙婂杩涚▼寮€閿€
          """
      
          def __init__(self, loader: 'DataLoader'):
              self.loader = loader
              self.indices = list(range(len(loader.dataset)))
              if loader.shuffle:
                  random.shuffle(self.indices)
              self.cursor = 0
              self.num_samples = len(self.indices)
      
          def __iter__(self):
              return self
      
          def __next__(self):
              """杩斿洖涓嬩竴涓猙atch鏁版嵁"""
              if self.cursor >= self.num_samples:
                  raise StopIteration
      
              remaining = self.num_samples - self.cursor
              if remaining < self.loader.batch_size and self.loader.drop_last:
                  raise StopIteration
      
              current_batch_size = min(self.loader.batch_size, remaining)
              batch_indices = self.indices[self.cursor:self.cursor + current_batch_size]
              self.cursor += current_batch_size
      
              batch_data, batch_target = self._load_batch(batch_indices)
              return batch_data, batch_target
      
          def _load_batch(self, indices):
              """鏍规嵁绱㈠紩鍒楄〃鍔犺浇涓€涓猙atch鐨勬暟鎹?""
              batch_data = []
              batch_target = []
              for idx in indices:
                  data, target = self.loader.dataset[idx]
                  batch_data.append(data)
                  batch_target.append(target)
              return Tensor.stack(batch_data), Tensor.stack(batch_target)
      
      
      class _AsyncLoaderIter:
          """
          寮傛鍔犺浇杩唬鍣?褰搉um_workers>0鏃朵娇鐢?
      
          浣跨敤澶氫釜瀛愯繘绋嬪苟琛屽姞杞芥暟鎹?閫氳繃Queue杩涜杩涚▼闂撮€氫俊
          """
      
          def __init__(self, loader: 'DataLoader'):
              self.loader = loader
              self.num_workers = loader.num_workers
              self.prefetch_factor = loader.prefetch_factor
      
              #鐢熸垚褰撳墠epoch鐨勭储寮曢『搴?
              indices=list(range(len(loader.dataset)))
              if loader.shuffle:
                  random.shuffle(indices)
      
              #璁＄畻鎬荤殑鎵规鏁?
              if loader.drop_last:
                  total_batches = len(indices) // loader.batch_size
              else:
                  total_batches = (len(indices) + loader.batch_size - 1) // loader.batch_size
      
              #灏嗘壒娆″垎閰嶇粰鍚勪釜worker(鎸夎繛缁壒娆″垝鍒?
              batches_per_worker = (total_batches + self.num_workers - 1) // self.num_workers
              self.workers=[]
              self.output_queue=Queue(maxsize=self.prefetch_factor*self.num_workers)
      
              for w in range(self.num_workers):
                  start_batch = w * batches_per_worker
                  end_batch = min(start_batch + batches_per_worker, total_batches)
                  if start_batch>=end_batch:
                      continue
      
                  worker_indices=[]
                  for batch_idx in range(start_batch,end_batch):
                      batch_start=batch_idx*loader.batch_size
                      batch_end=min(batch_start+loader.batch_size,len(indices))
                      worker_indices.extend(indices[batch_start:batch_end])
      
                  p = Process(target=_worker_loop, args=(
                      loader.dataset,
                      worker_indices,
                      loader.batch_size,
                      loader.drop_last,
                      self.output_queue
                  ))
                  p.start()
                  self.workers.append(p)
      
              self._batches_yielded = 0
              self._total_batches = total_batches
              self._workers_finished = 0
              self._shutdown = False
      
          def __len__(self):
              return self._total_batches
      
          def __iter__(self):
              return self
      
          def __next__(self):
              """
              鑾峰彇涓嬩竴涓彲鐢ㄧ殑batch鏁版嵁
      
              浼樺厛浠庡凡鏈夋暟鎹殑闃熷垪涓彇锛岃嫢閮戒负绌哄垯闃诲绛夊緟
              """
              if self._batches_yielded >= self._total_batches:
                  self._cleanup()
                  raise StopIteration
      
              while True:
                  result = self.output_queue.get()
                  if result is None:
                      self._workers_finished += 1
                      if self._workers_finished == len(self.workers):
                          self._cleanup()
                          raise StopIteration
                      continue
                  self._batches_yielded += 1
                  return result
      
          def _cleanup(self):
              """
              娓呯悊鎵€鏈夊瓙杩涚▼锛岄噴鏀捐祫婧?
      
              渚濇鍙戦€佺粓姝俊鍙枫€佺瓑寰呴€€鍑猴紝鑻ヨ秴鏃跺垯寮哄埗鏉€姝昏繘绋?
              """
              if self._shutdown:
                  return
              self._shutdown = True
              for p in self.workers:
                  if p in self.workers:
                     p.terminate()
                     p.join(timeout=1)
                     if p.is_alive():
                      p.kill()
      
          def __del__(self):
              """鏋愭瀯鏃剁‘淇濊繘绋嬭姝ｇ‘娓呯悊"""
              self._cleanup()
      
      class DataLoader:
          def __init__(self, dataset: Dataset, batch_size: int = 1, shuffle: bool = False, drop_last: bool = False
                       ,num_workers:int=0,prefetch_factor:int=2) -> None:
              self.dataset = dataset
              self.batch_size = batch_size
              self.shuffle = shuffle
              self.drop_last = drop_last
              self.num_workers = num_workers
              self.prefetch_factor = prefetch_factor
      
          def __iter__(self):
              if self.num_workers == 0:
                  return _SyncLoaderIter(self)
              else:
                  return _AsyncLoaderIter(self)
      
          def __len__(self):
              if self.drop_last:
                  return len(self.dataset) // self.batch_size
              else:
                  return (len(self.dataset) + self.batch_size - 1) // self.batch_size

      #渚濇棫鏄痠m2col鐨勪究鎹峰嚱鏁?
      def im2col(x, kernel_size, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), to_matrix=True):
          #     鍙傛暟璇存槑
          # x (dezero.Variable 鎴?ndarray): 杈撳叆鍙橀噺锛屽舰鐘朵负 (N, C, H, W)
          # kernel_size (int 鎴?(int, int)): 鍗风Н鏍稿ぇ灏?
          # stride (int 鎴?(int, int)): 鍗风Н鏍告闀?
          # pad (int 鎴?(int, int)): 杈撳叆鏁扮粍鐨勭┖闂村～鍏呭搴?
          # to_matrix (bool): 濡傛灉涓篢rue锛宑ol灏嗚閲嶅涓?D鏁扮粍锛屽舰鐘朵负 (N*OH*OW, C*KH*KW)
          # 杩斿洖鍊?
          # dezero.Variable: 杈撳嚭鍙橀噺銆傚鏋渢o_matrix涓篎alse锛岃緭鍑哄舰鐘朵负 (N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW)锛涘惁鍒欎负 (N*OH*OW, C*KH*KW)
          # 绗﹀彿璇存槑
          # N: 鎵规澶у皬
          # C: 杈撳叆閫氶亾鏁?
          # H 鍜?W: 杈撳叆鍥惧儚鐨勯珮搴﹀拰瀹藉害
          # KH 鍜?KW: 婊ゆ尝鍣ㄧ殑楂樺害鍜屽搴?
          # SH 鍜?SW: 婊ゆ尝鍣ㄧ殑姝ラ暱
          # PH 鍜?PW: 绌洪棿濉厖澶у皬
          # OH 鍜?OW: 杈撳嚭鐨勯珮搴﹀拰瀹藉害
          y = Im2col(kernel_size, stride, pad, to_matrix)(x)
          return y
      
      
      class Col2im(Function):
          def __init__(self, input_shape, kernel_size, stride, pad, to_matrix,visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.input_shape = input_shape
              self.kernel_size = kernel_size
              self.stride = stride
              self.pad = pad
              self.to_matrix = to_matrix
              self.visualize = visualize
      
      
          def forward(self, *xs):
              x = xs[0]
              y = col2im_array(x, self.input_shape, self.kernel_size, self.stride,
                               self.pad, self.to_matrix)
              return y
      
          def backward(self, gys):
              gx = im2col(gys, self.kernel_size, self.stride, self.pad,
                          self.to_matrix)
              return gx
      
      
      def col2im(x, input_shape, kernel_size, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), to_matrix=True):
          return Col2im(input_shape, kernel_size, stride, pad, to_matrix)(x)
      
      def im2col_array(img, kernel_size, stride, pad, to_matrix=True, dilation=1, xp=None):
          """
          灏嗗浘鍍忚浆鎹负鍒楃煩闃碉紙im2col鎿嶄綔锛夛紝鐢ㄤ簬鍗风Н鎿嶄綔銆?
      
          Args:
              img: 杈撳叆鍥惧儚锛屽舰鐘朵负 (N, C, H, W) 鐨?D寮犻噺
              kernel_size: 鍗风Н鏍稿ぇ灏?
              stride: 姝ラ暱
              pad: 濉厖澶у皬
              to_matrix: 鏄惁灏嗚緭鍑鸿浆鎹负鐭╅樀褰㈠紡
              dilation: 鎵╁紶鐜囷紙绌烘礊鍗风Н锛?
              xp: 鎸囧畾鏁扮粍妯″潡锛坣umpy鎴朿upy锛夛紝濡傛灉涓篘one鍒欒嚜鍔ㄤ粠img鎺ㄦ柇
      
          Returns:
              杞崲鍚庣殑鍒楃煩闃?
      
          Notes:
              - 鏀寔CPU锛坣umpy锛夊拰GPU锛坈upy锛変袱绉嶅悗绔?
              - 褰撴寚瀹歺p鍙傛暟鏃讹紝浼樺厛浣跨敤鎸囧畾鐨勬暟缁勬ā鍧楄繘琛岃绠?
              - 杩欏浜嶨PU璁粌鑷冲叧閲嶈锛屽洜涓鸿緭鍏mg鍙兘鏄痭umpy鏁扮粍鑰屾潈閲峎鏄痗upy鏁扮粍
              - 濡傛灉xp涓巌mg鐨勫疄闄呯被鍨嬩笉鍖归厤锛岄渶瑕佸厛灏唅mg杞崲涓簒p瀵瑰簲鐨勭被鍨?
          """
          # 濡傛灉浼犲叆鐨勬槸 Tensor锛屾彁鍙栧簳灞傛暟鎹?
          if isinstance(img, Tensor):
              img = img.data
      
          N, C, H, W = img.shape
          KH, KW = pair(kernel_size)
          SH, SW = pair(stride)
          PH, PW = pair(pad)
          DH, DW = pair(dilation)
          OH = get_conv_outsize(H, KH, SH, PH, DH)
          OW = get_conv_outsize(W, KW, SW, PW, DW)
      
          # 鍏抽敭淇锛氫紭鍏堜娇鐢ㄤ紶鍏ョ殑xp鍙傛暟锛岃€岄潪浠巌mg鎺ㄦ柇
          # 杩欐牱鍙互纭繚褰搃mg鏄痭umpy浣嗘潈閲嶆槸cupy鏃讹紝浣跨敤cupy杩涜璁＄畻
          if xp is None:
              xp = get_array_module(img)
          else:
              # 褰撴寚瀹氫簡xp浣唅mg绫诲瀷涓嶅尮閰嶆椂锛岄渶瑕佸厛灏唅mg杞崲涓簒p瀵瑰簲鐨勭被鍨?
              # 娉ㄦ剰锛歩mg鍙兘鏄疶ensor瀵硅薄锛岄渶瑕佸厛鎻愬彇鍏禿ata
              img_data = img.data if isinstance(img, Tensor) else img
              img_xp = get_array_module(img_data)
              if img_xp != xp:
                  # 鏍规嵁鐩爣xp妯″潡姝ｇ‘杞崲鏁扮粍绫诲瀷
                  # 褰搙p鏄痭umpy浣唅mg鏄痗upy鏃讹紝闇€瑕佷娇鐢╟upy.asnumpy()
                  # 褰搙p鏄痗upy浣唅mg鏄痭umpy鏃讹紝闇€瑕佷娇鐢╟upy.asarray()
                  if xp is np:
                      # 鐩爣xp鏄痭umpy锛屾簮鏄痗upy
                      img_data = cp.asnumpy(img_data)
                  else:
                      # 鐩爣xp鏄痗upy锛屾簮鏄痭umpy
                      img_data = xp.asarray(img_data)
              img = img_data
      
          # CPU/GPU瀹炵幇锛氫娇鐢∟umPy/CuPy杩涜濉厖鍜宲atch鎻愬彇
          img = xp.pad(img,
                       ((0, 0), (0, 0), (PH, PH + SH - 1), (PW, PW + SW - 1)),
                       mode='constant', constant_values=(0,))
          col = xp.ndarray((N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW), dtype=img.dtype)
      
          use_tiled = _use_cpu_tiled_kernel(xp, OH, OW)
          if use_tiled:
              tile_h, tile_w = _select_cpu_tile_shape(
                  N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW, img.dtype.itemsize, _CPU_IM2COL_TILE
              )
              for oh0 in range(0, OH, tile_h):
                  oh1 = OH if (oh0 + tile_h) > OH else (oh0 + tile_h)
                  h_base = oh0 * SH
                  oh_span = oh1 - oh0
                  for ow0 in range(0, OW, tile_w):
                      ow1 = OW if (ow0 + tile_w) > OW else (ow0 + tile_w)
                      w_base = ow0 * SW
                      ow_span = ow1 - ow0
                      for j in range(KH):
                          j_base = j * DH + h_base
                          j_lim = j_base + SH * oh_span
                          for i in range(KW):
                              i_base = i * DW + w_base
                              i_lim = i_base + SW * ow_span
                              col[:, :, j, i, oh0:oh1, ow0:ow1] = img[:, :, j_base:j_lim:SH, i_base:i_lim:SW]
          else:

              for j in range(KH):
                  j_lim = j * DH + SH * OH
                  for i in range(KW):
                      i_lim = i * DW + SW * OW
                      col[:, :, j, i, :, :] = img[:, :, j*DH:j_lim:SH, i*DW:i_lim:SW]
      
          if to_matrix:
              col = col.transpose((0, 4, 5, 1, 2, 3)).reshape((N * OH * OW, -1))
      
          return col
      
      
      def col2im_array(col, img_shape, kernel_size, stride, pad, to_matrix=True, dilation=(1,1)):
          N, C, H, W = img_shape
          KH, KW = pair(kernel_size)
          SH, SW = pair(stride)
          PH, PW = pair(pad)
          DH, DW = pair(dilation)
          OH = get_conv_outsize(H, KH, SH, PH, DH)
          OW = get_conv_outsize(W, KW, SW, PW, DW)
      
          # Ensure `col` has shape (N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW)
          if to_matrix:
              col = col.reshape(N, OH, OW, C, KH, KW).transpose(0, 3, 4, 5, 1, 2)
      
          xp = get_array_module(col)
      
          img = xp.zeros((N, C, H + 2 * PH + SH - 1, W + 2 * PW + SW - 1),
                         dtype=col.dtype)
          use_tiled = _use_cpu_tiled_kernel(xp, OH, OW)
          if use_tiled:
              tile_h, tile_w = _select_cpu_tile_shape(
                  N, C, KH, KW, OH, OW, col.dtype.itemsize, _CPU_COL2IM_TILE
              )
              for oh0 in range(0, OH, tile_h):
                  oh1 = OH if (oh0 + tile_h) > OH else (oh0 + tile_h)
                  h_base = oh0 * SH
                  oh_span = oh1 - oh0
                  for ow0 in range(0, OW, tile_w):
                      ow1 = OW if (ow0 + tile_w) > OW else (ow0 + tile_w)
                      w_base = ow0 * SW
                      ow_span = ow1 - ow0
                      for j in range(KH):
                          j_base = j * DH + h_base
                          j_lim = j_base + SH * oh_span
                          for i in range(KW):
                              i_base = i * DW + w_base
                              i_lim = i_base + SW * ow_span
                              img[:, :, j_base:j_lim:SH, i_base:i_lim:SW] += col[:, :, j, i, oh0:oh1, ow0:ow1]
          else:
              #閫傚悎灏忓浘鍍忓鐞?
              # for oh in range(OH):
              #     for ow in range(OW):
              #         # 鑰冭檻濉厖鍋忕Щ

              #         img[:, :, j_start:j_start+KH, i_start:i_start+KW] += col[:, :, :, :, oh, ow]
              for j in range(KH):
                  j_lim = j * DH + SH * OH
                  for i in range(KW):
                      i_lim = i * DW + SW * OW
                      img[:, :, j*DH:j_lim:SH, i*DW:i_lim:SW] += col[:, :, j, i, :, :]
          return img[:, :, PH:H + PH, PW:W + PW]

      class Conv2d(Function):
          WINOGRAD_MIN_INPUT_SHAPE = (12, 12, 256, 256)

              OW = get_conv_outsize(W_in, KW, SW, PW, DW)

              # 杩欓噷涓嶅洖閫€鍒?im2col锛涗粎浼樺寲 Winograd 鏈矾寰勩€?

                  T[:, :, 2, :] = half * (g0 - g1 + g2)

                  U = xp.empty((OC, C, 4, 4), dtype=calc_dtype)

              L = workspace['L']

              L[..., 1, :] = d1 + d2
              L[..., 2, :] = d2 - d1
              L[..., 3, :] = d1 - d3
      
              V = workspace['V']
              V[..., :, 0] = L[..., :, 0] - L[..., :, 2]
              V[..., :, 1] = L[..., :, 1] + L[..., :, 2]
              V[..., :, 2] = L[..., :, 2] - L[..., :, 1]
              V[..., :, 3] = L[..., :, 1] - L[..., :, 3]

              r0 = M[..., 0, :] + M[..., 1, :] + M[..., 2, :]
              r1 = M[..., 1, :] - M[..., 2, :] - M[..., 3, :]
      
              Y00 = r0[..., 0] + r0[..., 1] + r0[..., 2]
              Y01 = r0[..., 1] - r0[..., 2] - r0[..., 3]
              Y10 = r1[..., 0] + r1[..., 1] + r1[..., 2]
              Y11 = r1[..., 1] - r1[..., 2] - r1[..., 3]
      
              y_buffer = workspace['y_buffer']
              y_buffer[:, :, 0::2, 0::2] = Y00
              y_buffer[:, :, 0::2, 1::2] = Y01
              y_buffer[:, :, 1::2, 0::2] = Y10
              y_buffer[:, :, 1::2, 1::2] = Y11
      
              y_res = y_buffer[:, :, :out_h, :out_w]
      
              if b is not None:

                          b_val = xp.array(b_val)
                      y_res += b_val.reshape(1, -1, 1, 1)
      
              return y_res.astype(dtype, copy=False)
      
      def conv2d(x, W, b=None, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1, visualize=False):
          return Conv2d(stride, pad, dilation, visualize)(x, W, b)
      
      
      def conv2d_backward_input_array(gy, W, stride=(1, 1), pad=(0, 0), dilation=(1, 1), out_h=None, out_w=None):
          """璁＄畻鍗风Н瀵硅緭鍏?x 鐨勬搴︼紙鏀寔 dilation锛夈€?
      
          Args:
              gy: 涓婃父姊害锛屽舰鐘?(N, OC, OH, OW)
              W: 鍗风Н鏍革紝褰㈢姸 (OC, C, KH, KW)
          """
          SH, SW = pair(stride)
          PH, PW = pair(pad)
          DH, DW = pair(dilation)
      
          N, OC, OH, OW = gy.shape
          OC_W, C, KH, KW = W.shape
          assert OC == OC_W
      
          if out_h is None or out_w is None:
              out_h = SH * (OH - 1) - 2 * PH + DH * (KH - 1) + 1
              out_w = SW * (OW - 1) - 2 * PW + DW * (KW - 1) + 1
      
          xp = get_array_module(gy)
      
          gx_pad = xp.zeros((N, C, out_h + 2 * PH + SH - 1, out_w + 2 * PW + SW - 1), dtype=gy.dtype)

      class Conv2d(Function):
          WINOGRAD_MIN_INPUT_SHAPE = (12, 12, 256, 256)
          FFT_MIN_KERNEL_SIZE = 5
          FFT_MIN_SPATIAL_SIZE = 32
          AUTOTUNE_ENABLED = True
          AUTOTUNE_TRAIN_ONLY = True
          _path_cache = {}

          def __init__(self, stride=(1,1), pad=(0,0), dilation=1, visualize=False):
              super().__init__()
              self.stride = pair(stride)
              self.pad = pair(pad)
              self.dilation = pair(dilation)
              self.visualize = visualize
      
          def _gemm_output_shape(self, x, W):
              KH, KW = W.shape[2:]
              SH, SW = self.stride
              PH, PW = self.pad
              DH, DW = self.dilation
              N, _, H, W_in = x.shape

              OW = get_conv_outsize(W_in, KW, SW, PW, DW)
              return N, OC, KH, KW, OH, OW
      
      
      
          def _select_forward_path(self, x, W):
              """閫夋嫨鍓嶅悜璺緞: winograd | fft | gemm | im2col銆?""
              if len(x.shape) != 4 or len(W.shape) != 4:
                  return 'im2col'
      
              N, C, H, W_in = x.shape
              _, _, KH, KW = W.shape
              SH, SW = self.stride
              DH, DW = self.dilation
              min_n, min_c, min_h, min_w = self.WINOGRAD_MIN_INPUT_SHAPE
              is_large_input = N >= min_n and C >= min_c and H >= min_h and W_in >= min_w
      
              # 3x3 stride=1 dilation=1 涓斿ぇ鍥句紭鍏?Winograd
              if KH == 3 and KW == 3 and SH == 1 and SW == 1 and DH == 1 and DW == 1 and is_large_input:
                  return 'winograd'
      
              # 澶ф牳鍗风Н鍦ㄨ緝澶х┖闂村昂瀵镐笂浼樺厛 FFT
              # 淇濇寔淇濆畧锛氬彧鍦ㄩ潪绌烘礊鍗风Н鏃跺惎鐢紝閬垮厤璺緞閫夋嫨杩囨棭鍋忓悜 FFT
              if DH == 1 and DW == 1:
                  is_fft_kernel = (KH if KH >= KW else KW) >= self.FFT_MIN_KERNEL_SIZE
                  is_fft_input = H >= self.FFT_MIN_SPATIAL_SIZE and W_in >= self.FFT_MIN_SPATIAL_SIZE
                  if is_fft_kernel and is_fft_input:

              if is_large_input:
